						has_state_flag = TUR_manastir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_albanian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_albanian_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_manastir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_albanian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_albanian_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = TUR_albanian_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir }
			if = { #MANASTIR SUNNI TURKISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_manastir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_manastir
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir = 0 }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_manastir = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_manastir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_manastir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_manastir_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_manastir }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_manastir = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_manastir_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_manastir }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_minority_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_manastir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_minority_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir }
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_manastir = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_manastir = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir = 2 }
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_manastir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_manastir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_manastir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_manastir }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_manastir = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_manastir }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_manastir }
	}
}

					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yanya = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_yanya = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_yanya = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_yanya = 2 }
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yanya = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yanya = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_yanya }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yanya = ROOT.TUR_albanian_orthodox_minority_value_yanya }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yanya = ROOT.TUR_albanian_sunni_minority_value_yanya }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yanya = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yanya = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yanya }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yanya }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_selanik = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #SELANIK
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_selanik
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #SELANIK ORTHODOX GREEKS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_selanik
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_selanik
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik }
			if = { #SELANIK ORTHODOX BULGARIANS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_selanik
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_bulgarian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_selanik
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_bulgarian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik }
			if = { #SELANIK JUDAIC JEWS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_selanik
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_jewish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_jewish_judaic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_selanik
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_jewish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_jewish_judaic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik = TUR_jewish_judaic_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik = TUR_judaic_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik }
			if = { #SELANIK SUNNI TURKISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_selanik
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_selanik
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_selanik = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_selanik = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_selanik = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_selanik = ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_selanik = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_selanik = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_selanik_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_selanik = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_selanik }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_selanik = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_selanik_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_selanik }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_selanik = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_selanik = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_selanik }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_selanik = ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_selanik }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_selanik = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_selanik }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_selanik = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_selanik }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_selanik = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_selanik = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_selanik }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_selanik }
	}
}

					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_edirne = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_edirne = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_edirne = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_edirne = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_edirne }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_edirne = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_edirne }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_edirne = ROOT.TUR_bulgarian_orthodox_minority_value_edirne }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_edirne = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_edirne }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_edirne = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_edirne }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_edirne = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_edirne = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_edirne }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_edirne }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_catalca = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #CATALCA
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_catalca
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #CATALCA ORTHODOX GREEKS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_catalca
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_catalca = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_catalca
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_catalca = TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_catalca = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_catalca = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_catalca = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_catalca = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_catalca }
			if = { #CATALCA SUNNI TURKISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_catalca
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_catalca = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_catalca
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_catalca = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_catalca = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_catalca = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_catalca = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_catalca = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_catalca }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_catalca = 0 }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_catalca = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_catalca }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_catalca = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_catalca }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_catalca = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_catalca = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_catalca }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_catalca }
	}
}

						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_archipelago = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_archipelago = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_archipelago = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_archipelago = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_archipelago }
			if = { #ARCHIPELAGO SUNNI TURKISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_archipelago
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_archipelago
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_archipelago = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_archipelago = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_archipelago = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_archipelago }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_archipelago = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_archipelago_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_archipelago = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_archipelago }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_archipelago = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_archipelago_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_archipelago }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_archipelago = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_archipelago = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_archipelago = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_archipelago }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_archipelago = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_archipelago }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_archipelago = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_archipelago = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_archipelago }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_archipelago }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_cyprus = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #CYPRUS
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_cyprus
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #CYPRUS ORTHODOX GREEKS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_cyprus
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_cyprus = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_cyprus
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_cyprus = TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_cyprus = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_cyprus = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_cyprus = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_cyprus = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_cyprus }
			if = { #CYPRUS SUNNI TURKISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_cyprus
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_cyprus = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_cyprus
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_cyprus = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_cyprus = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_cyprus = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_cyprus = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_cyprus = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_cyprus }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_cyprus = 0 }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_cyprus = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_cyprus }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_cyprus = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_cyprus }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_cyprus = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_cyprus = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_cyprus }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_cyprus }
	}

								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_hudavendigar = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_hudavendigar = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_hudavendigar = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_hudavendigar = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_hudavendigar }
			if = { #HUDAVENDIGAR SUNNI TURKISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_hudavendigar
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_hudavendigar
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_hudavendigar = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_hudavendigar = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_hudavendigar = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_hudavendigar }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_hudavendigar = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_hudavendigar }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_hudavendigar = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_hudavendigar_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_hudavendigar = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_hudavendigar }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_hudavendigar = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_hudavendigar_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_hudavendigar }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_hudavendigar = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_hudavendigar = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_hudavendigar = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_hudavendigar = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_hudavendigar }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_hudavendigar = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_hudavendigar }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_hudavendigar = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_hudavendigar }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_hudavendigar = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_hudavendigar = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_hudavendigar }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_hudavendigar }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_aydin = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #AYDIN
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_aydin
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #AYDIN ORTHODOX GREEKS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_aydin
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_aydin = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_aydin
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_aydin = TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_aydin = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_aydin = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_aydin = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_aydin = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_aydin }
			if = { #AYDIN SUNNI TURKISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_aydin
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aydin = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_aydin
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aydin = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aydin = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aydin = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aydin = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aydin = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aydin }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aydin = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aydin = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aydin = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_aydin }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aydin = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aydin }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_aydin_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_aydin = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aydin }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_aydin = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_aydin_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_aydin }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aydin = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_aydin = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aydin = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aydin = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_aydin }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aydin = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aydin }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aydin = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aydin = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aydin }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aydin }
	}
}

				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_ankara = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_ankara = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_ankara }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_ankara = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_ankara = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_ankara = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_ankara }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_ankara = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_ankara }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_ankara = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_ankara }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_ankara_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_ankara = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_ankara }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_ankara = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_ankara_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_ankara }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_ankara = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_ankara = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_ankara = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_ankara = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_ankara = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_ankara }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_ankara = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_ankara }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_ankara = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_ankara }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_ankara = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_ankara = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_ankara }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_ankara }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_konya = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #KONYA
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_konya
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #KONYA APOSTOLIC ARMENIAN
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_konya
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_armenian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_konya = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_konya
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_armenian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_konya = TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_konya = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_konya = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_konya = TUR_apostolic_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_konya = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_konya }
			if = { #KONYA ORTHODOX GREEK
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_konya
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_konya = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_konya
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_greek_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_konya = TUR_greek_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_konya = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_konya = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_konya = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_konya = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_konya }
			if = { #KONYA SUNNI TURKISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_konya
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_konya = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_konya
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_konya = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_konya = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_konya = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_konya = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_konya = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_konya }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_konya = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_konya = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_konya = ROOT.TUR_apostolic_armenian_minority_value_konya }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_konya = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_konya }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_konya = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_konya }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_konya_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_konya = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_konya }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_konya = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_konya_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_konya }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_konya = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_apostolic_armenian_minority_value_konya = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_konya = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_konya = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_konya = ROOT.TUR_apostolic_armenian_minority_value_konya }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_konya = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_konya }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_konya = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_konya }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_konya = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_konya = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_konya }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_konya }
	}
}

				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_sivas
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_shia_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_sivas
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_shia_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas = TUR_turkish_shia_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas = TUR_shia_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_sivas = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_sivas = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_sivas = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_sivas }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_sivas = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_sivas }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_sivas = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_sivas }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_sivas = ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_sivas_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_sivas = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_sivas }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_sivas = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_sivas_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_sivas }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_sivas = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_sivas = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_sivas = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_sivas = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_sivas = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_sivas }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_sivas = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_sivas }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_sivas = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_sivas }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_sivas = ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_sivas }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_sivas = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_sivas = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_sivas }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_sivas }
	}
}

				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_erzurum = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_erzurum = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_erzurum }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_erzurum = ROOT.TUR_georgian_orthodox_minority_value_erzurum }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_erzurum = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_erzurum = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_erzurum_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_erzurum = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_erzurum }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_erzurum = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_erzurum_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_erzurum }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_erzurum = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_erzurum = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_georgian_orthodox_minority_value_erzurum = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_erzurum = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_erzurum }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_erzurum = ROOT.TUR_georgian_orthodox_minority_value_erzurum }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_erzurum = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_erzurum = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_erzurum }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_erzurum = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_erzurum = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_erzurum }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_erzurum }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_mamuret_ul_aziz = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #MAMURET UL AZIZ
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_mamuret_ul_aziz
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #MAMURET UL AZIZ APOSTOLIC ARMENIANS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_mamuret_ul_aziz
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_armenian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_mamuret_ul_aziz
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_armenian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = TUR_apostolic_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz }
			if = { #MAMURET UL AZIZ SUNNI KURDISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_mamuret_ul_aziz
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_kurdish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_mamuret_ul_aziz
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_kurdish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz }
			if = { #MAMURET UL AZIZ SUNNI TURKISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_mamuret_ul_aziz
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes

						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }

					if = {

							OR = {

							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 0 }
					}
					if = {

						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 2 }

						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes

							OR = {

							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = TUR_shia_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_shia_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_mamuret_ul_aziz_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_mamuret_ul_aziz }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_mamuret_ul_aziz_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_mamuret_ul_aziz }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = -0.005 }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_mamuret_ul_aziz = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mamuret_ul_aziz }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_mamuret_ul_aziz }
	}

				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_diyarbakir = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_diyarbakir_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_diyarbakir = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_diyarbakir }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_diyarbakir = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_diyarbakir_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_diyarbakir }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_diyarbakir = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_diyarbakir = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_diyarbakir = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_diyarbakir = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_diyarbakir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_diyarbakir = ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_diyarbakir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_diyarbakir = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_diyarbakir = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_diyarbakir }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_diyarbakir = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_diyarbakir = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_diyarbakir }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_diyarbakir }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_bitlis = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #BITLIS
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_bitlis
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #BITLIS APOSTOLIC ARMENIANS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_bitlis
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_armenian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_bitlis = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_bitlis
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_armenian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_bitlis = TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_bitlis = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_bitlis = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_bitlis = TUR_apostolic_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_bitlis = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_bitlis }
			if = { #BITLIS SUNNI KURDISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_bitlis
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_kurdish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_bitlis
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_kurdish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis = TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_bitlis = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_bitlis = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_bitlis = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_bitlis }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_bitlis = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_bitlis_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_bitlis = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_bitlis }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_bitlis = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_bitlis_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_bitlis }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_bitlis = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_bitlis = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_bitlis = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_bitlis }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_bitlis = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_bitlis }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_bitlis = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_bitlis = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_bitlis }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_bitlis }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_van = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #VAN
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_van
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #VAN APOSTOLIC ARMENIANS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_van
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_armenian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_van = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_van
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_armenian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_van = TUR_armenian_apostolic_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_van = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_van = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_van = TUR_apostolic_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_van = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_van }
			if = { #VAN SYRIAC ASSYRIANS
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_van
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_assyrian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_assyrian_syriac_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_van = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_van
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_assyrian_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_assyrian_syriac_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_van = TUR_assyrian_syriac_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_van = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_van = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_van = TUR_syriac_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_van = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_van }
			if = { #VAN SUNNI KURDISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_van
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_kurdish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_van
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_kurdish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van = TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_van = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_van = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_van = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_van }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_van = ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_van }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_van = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_van_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_van = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_van }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_van = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_van_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_van }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_van = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_van = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_van = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_van = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_van }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_van = ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_van }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_van = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_van }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_van = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_van = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_van }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_van }
	}
}

							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_aleppo = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_aleppo = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_aleppo = TUR_shia_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_aleppo = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_aleppo }
			if = { #ALEPPO SUNNI KURDISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_aleppo
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_kurdish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_aleppo
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_kurdish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = TUR_kurdish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo }
			if = { #ALEPPO SUNNI TURKISH
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_aleppo
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_aleppo
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_turkish_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = TUR_turkish_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_aleppo }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_aleppo }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aleppo = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_aleppo }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_aleppo }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_aleppo }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_aleppo }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_aleppo_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aleppo }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_aleppo = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_aleppo_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_aleppo }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aleppo = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_aleppo = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_aleppo = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_aleppo = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_armenian_apostolic_minority_value_aleppo }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_greek_orthodox_minority_value_aleppo }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_aleppo }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_aleppo }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_aleppo }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aleppo = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_aleppo }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_aleppo }
	}
}

	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #MOSUL
			limit = {

					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_mosul
