			if = { #MOSUL APOSTOLIC ARMENIANS

				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mosul = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mosul }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mosul = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_mosul }

				set_variable = { global.TUR_mosul_max_centralization = 100 }

				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_mosul_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_mosul }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_mosul = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_mosul = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_mosul = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mosul = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_mosul = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mosul = ROOT.TUR_assyrian_syriac_minority_value_mosul }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mosul = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_mosul }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mosul = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_mosul }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mosul = ROOT.TUR_turkish_sunni_minority_value_mosul }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mosul = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_mosul = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_mosul }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_mosul }

				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_deir_ez_zor = 2 }
			}
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_deir_ez_zor = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_deir_ez_zor }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_deir_ez_zor = ROOT.TUR_kurdish_sunni_minority_value_deir_ez_zor }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_deir_ez_zor = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_deir_ez_zor = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_deir_ez_zor }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_deir_ez_zor }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_damascus = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #DAMASCUS
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_damascus
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #DAMASCUS SUNNI ARAB
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_damascus
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_damascus = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_damascus
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_damascus = TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_damascus = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_damascus = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_damascus = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_damascus = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_damascus }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_damascus = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_damascus = -0.005 }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_damascus = 2 }
			}
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_damascus = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_damascus }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_damascus = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_damascus = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_damascus }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_damascus }
	}
}

			if = { #BEIRUT JUDAIC JEWISH

			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_beirut }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }

				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_beirut }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_beirut }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_beirut_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_beirut }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_beirut = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_beirut_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_beirut }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_beirut }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_beirut }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_beirut }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_catholic_minority_value_beirut }
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_beirut = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_beirut = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_beirut = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_beirut = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_catholic_minority_value_beirut = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_beirut = 2 }
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_beirut }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_beirut }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_beirut }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_arab_catholic_minority_value_beirut }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_beirut }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_beirut = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_beirut }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_beirut }
	}
}

						check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_baghdad = TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_baghdad = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_baghdad = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_baghdad = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_baghdad = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_baghdad }
			if = { #BAGHDAD SUNNI ARAB
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_baghdad
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_shia_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_baghdad = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_baghdad
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_shia_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_baghdad = TUR_arab_shia_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_baghdad = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_baghdad = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_baghdad = TUR_shia_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_baghdad = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_baghdad }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_baghdad = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_baghdad = -0.005 }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_baghdad = 2 }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_baghdad = 2 }
			}
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_baghdad = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_baghdad }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_baghdad = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_baghdad }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_baghdad = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_baghdad }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_baghdad = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_baghdad }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_baghdad = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_baghdad = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_baghdad }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_baghdad }
	}
}

								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_yemen = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_yemen = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_yemen = TUR_judaic_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_yemen = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_yemen }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_yemen }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_yemen }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yemen = 0 }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yemen = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_yemen }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_yemen }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_yemen }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_yemen_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yemen }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_yemen = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_yemen_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_yemen }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_yemen }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_yemen }
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yemen = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_yemen = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_yemen = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_yemen = 2 }
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yemen = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_jewish_judaic_minority_value_yemen }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_yemen }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yemen = ROOT.TUR_arab_shia_minority_value_yemen }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yemen = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yemen = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_yemen }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_yemen }
	}
}

						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_suez = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_suez = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_suez = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_suez }
			if = { #SUEZ SUNNI ARAB
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_suez
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_suez = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_suez
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_suez = TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_suez = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_suez = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_suez = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_suez = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_suez }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_suez = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_suez }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_suez = ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_suez }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_suez = 0 }
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_suez = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_suez }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_suez = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_suez = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_suez }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_suez }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_alexandria = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #ALEXANDRIA
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_alexandria
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #ALEXANDRIA ORTHODOX ARAB
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_alexandria
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_alexandria = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_alexandria
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_alexandria = TUR_arab_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_alexandria = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_alexandria = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_alexandria = TUR_orthodox_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_alexandria = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_alexandria }
			if = { #ALEXANDRIA SUNNI ARAB
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_alexandria
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_alexandria = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_alexandria
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_alexandria = TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_alexandria = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_alexandria = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_alexandria = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_alexandria = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_alexandria }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_alexandria = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_alexandria = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_alexandria }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_alexandria = 0 }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_alexandria = ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_alexandria }
				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_alexandria = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_alexandria }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_alexandria_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_alexandria = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_alexandria }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_alexandria = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_alexandria_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_alexandria }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_alexandria = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_alexandria }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_minority_value_alexandria = ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_alexandria }
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_alexandria = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_alexandria = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_alexandria = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_alexandria = ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_alexandria }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_alexandria = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_alexandria }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_alexandria = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_alexandria = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_alexandria }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_alexandria }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_cairo = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #CAIRO
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_cairo
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #CAIRO ORTHODOX ARAB
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_cairo
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_cairo = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_cairo
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_orthodox_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_cairo = TUR_arab_orthodox_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_cairo = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_cairo = 2 }
					}

					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_cairo = TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable }

							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_cairo = 0 }

			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_cairo = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_autonomy_in_non_muslim_vilayets }

				#
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_cairo = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_cairo }
				#
				set_variable = { global.TUR_cairo_max_centralization = 100 }
				set_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_cairo = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_cairo }
				multiply_temp_variable = { TUR_christian_centralization_value_cairo = 1000 }
				add_to_variable = { global.TUR_cairo_max_centralization = TUR_christian_centralization_value_cairo }

				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
					set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_cairo = -0.005 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_cairo = 2 }
					divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_cairo = 2 }
				}
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_cairo = ROOT.TUR_arab_orthodox_minority_value_cairo }
				add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_cairo = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_cairo }

		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_cairo = 0 }

		}

						check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_khartoum = TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_khartoum = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_khartoum = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_khartoum = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_khartoum = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_khartoum }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_khartoum = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_khartoum = -0.005 }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_khartoum = 2 }
			}
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_khartoum = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_khartoum }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_khartoum = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_khartoum = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_khartoum }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_khartoum }
	}
}
TUR_minority_system_trablusgarp = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { #TRABLUSGARP
			limit = {
				any_state = {
					TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
					has_state_flag = TUR_trablusgarp
					TUR_state_has_minority_modifier = yes
				}
			}
			if = { #TRABLUSGARP SUNNI ARAB
				limit = {
					any_state = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_trablusgarp
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_trablusgarp = 0 }
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						TUR_is_controlled_by_us_or_committee_government = yes
						has_state_flag = TUR_trablusgarp
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_arab_minority }
						check_variable = { TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable < 0 }
					}
					add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_trablusgarp = TUR_arab_sunni_state_variable }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet_lebanon }
								has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_autonomous_vilayet }
							}
						}
						multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_trablusgarp = 0 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TUR_semi_autonomous_vilayet } }
						divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_trablusgarp = 2 }
					}
				}
				multiply_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_trablusgarp = TUR_sunni_population_factor_new }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_trablusgarp = 100 }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_trablusgarp }
			#CALCULATE TOTAL VALUE
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_trablusgarp = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TUR_adem_i_merkeziyetcilik }
				set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_trablusgarp = -0.005 }
				divide_variable = { ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_trablusgarp = 2 }
			}
			add_to_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_trablusgarp = ROOT.TUR_arab_sunni_minority_value_trablusgarp }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_minority_value_trablusgarp = 0 }
			set_variable = { ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_trablusgarp = 0 }
		}
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_variable = ROOT.TUR_minority_value_trablusgarp }
		add_to_variable = { TUR_sublime_realm_pp_variable = ROOT.TUR_political_power_value_trablusgarp }
	}
}
